            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy aria-label="Copy link" for="issue-3693532135-permalink" role="menuitem" data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">

</clipboard-copy>  <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true" class="sr-only" data-clipboard-copy-feedback></div>
</span>            <span data-view-component="true">
  <clipboard-copy role="menuitem" value="Fix the case where a node that removes all of the peers would be left with `membChanging` set permanently. This would prevent the single node cluster to admit new nodes back to form a bigger cluster. The `membChanging` flag prevents concurrent membership changes, and is reset after a membership change is committed. However, in the case of a single node cluster, entries are never committed and `memChanging` remains set.
The underlying problem is that single node clusters are not working properly: entries are committed only after tracking successful append entry responses from the followers. In a single node cluster there&#39;s no one that sends append entry responses, and the quorum checking is never done elsewhere.
The fix consists in extracting function `tryCommit` from `trackResponse`. `tryCommit` checks if there&#39;s a quorum for a given index, and if so it commits the corresponding entry. We call this function if, after proposing a peer remove, we are left with a single node cluster.

Signed-off-by: Daniele Sciascia &lt;danele@nats.io&gt;
" data-view-component="true" class="dropdown-item btn-link">

          <div>
        <div class="edit-comment-hide">
  
  <task-lists disabled sortable>
    <div class="comment-body markdown-body js-comment-body soft-wrap user-select-contain d-block">
      <p dir="auto">Fix the case where a node that removes all of the peers would be left with <code class="notranslate">membChanging</code> set permanently. This would prevent the single node cluster to admit new nodes back to form a bigger cluster. The <code class="notranslate">membChanging</code> flag prevents concurrent membership changes, and is reset after a membership change is committed. However, in the case of a single node cluster, entries are never committed and <code class="notranslate">memChanging</code> remains set.<br>
The underlying problem is that single node clusters are not working properly: entries are committed only after tracking successful append entry responses from the followers. In a single node cluster there's no one that sends append entry responses, and the quorum checking is never done elsewhere.<br>
The fix consists in extracting function <code class="notranslate">tryCommit</code> from <code class="notranslate">trackResponse</code>. <code class="notranslate">tryCommit</code> checks if there's a quorum for a given index, and if so it commits the corresponding entry. We call this function if, after proposing a peer remove, we are left with a single node cluster.</p>

<p dir="auto">Signed-off-by: Daniele Sciascia <a href="mailto:danele@nats.io">danele@nats.io</a></p>
    </div>
  </task-lists>
  
</div>

      </div>

      <div class="js-timeline-item js-timeline-progressive-focus-container" data-gid="C_kwDOAGJRq9oAKDVhOThiY2E2NzVmNjM4Mzk1N2FiOTZkZjgzNTAyZjBiNTg4NWJhZDI">
  
        <div>
  <div >
      <div data-view-component="true" class="TimelineItem">
  
  <div data-view-component="true" class="TimelineItem-badge"><svg aria-hidden="true" data-component="Octicon" height="16" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" data-view-component="true" class="octicon octicon-git-commit">
    <path d="M11.93 8.5a4.002 4.002 0 0 1-7.86 0H.75a.75.75 0 0 1 0-1.5h3.32a4.002 4.002 0 0 1 7.86 0h3.32a.75.75 0 0 1 0 1.5Zm-1.43-.75a2.5 2.5 0 1 0-5 0 2.5 2.5 0 0 0 5 0Z"></path>
</svg></div>
  <div data-view-component="true" class="TimelineItem-body">          <div class="js-details-container Details js-socket-channel js-updatable-content">
  <div class="d-flex flex-md-row flex-column">
    <div class="d-flex flex-auto">
      
<div class="AvatarStack flex-self-start  " >
  <div class="AvatarStack-body" >
        <a class="avatar avatar-user" style="width:20px;height:20px;" data-test-selector="commits-avatar-stack-avatar-link" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/sciascid/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="/sciascid">
          <img data-test-selector="commits-avatar-stack-avatar-image" src="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/3881727?s=40&amp;v=4" width="20" height="20" alt="@sciascid" class=" avatar-user" />
</a>  </div>
</div>

      <div class="pr-1 flex-auto min-width-0" >
        <code>
          <a title="NRG: peer removing all followers leaves membership in progress

Fix the case where a node that removes all of the peers would be
left with `membChanging` set permanently. This would prevent the
single node cluster to admit new nodes back to form a bigger
cluster. The `membChanging` flag prevents concurrent membership
changes, and is reset after a membership change is committed.
However, in the case of a single node cluster, entries are
never committed and `memChanging` remains set.
The underlying problem is that single node clusters are not
working properly: entries are committed only after tracking
successful append entry responses from the followers. In a single
node cluster there&#39;s no one that sends append entry responses,
and the quorum checking is never done elsewhere.
The fix consists in extracting function `tryCommit` from
`trackResponse`. `tryCommit` checks if there&#39;s a quorum for a
given index, and if so it commits the corresponding entry. We
call this function if, after proposing a peer remove, we are
left with a single node cluster.

Signed-off-by: Daniele Sciascia &lt;daniele@nats.io&gt;" data-pjax="true" class="Link--secondary markdown-title" href="/nats-io/nats-server/pull/7609/commits/5a98bca675f6383957ab96df83502f0b5885bad2">NRG: peer removing all followers leaves membership in progress</a>
        </code>

          <span class="hidden-text-expander inline">
              <button aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Commit message body" type="button" data-view-component="true" class="ellipsis-expander js-details-target btn">    &hellip;
</button>          </span>
      </div>

      <div class="pr-1 d-md-inline-block d-none">
        
<batch-deferred-content class="d-inline-block" data-url="/commits/badges">
    <input type="hidden" name="id" value="C_kwDOAGJRq9oAKDVhOThiY2E2NzVmNjM4Mzk1N2FiOTZkZjgzNTAyZjBiNTg4NWJhZDI" data-targets="batch-deferred-content.inputs" autocomplete="off" />

    <span class="Skeleton d-inline-block mr-1" style="width:75px; height:14px; margin-bottom:-4px;"></span>

</batch-deferred-content>
      </div>

      <div class="pr-1 flex-shrink-0" style="width: 16px;">
        <div class="js-socket-channel js-updatable-content" data-channel="eyJjIjoicmVwbzo2NDQzNDM1OmNvbW1pdDo1YTk4YmNhNjc1ZjYzODM5NTdhYjk2ZGY4MzUwMmYwYjU4ODViYWQyIiwidCI6MTc4ODgzMzIyNX0=--dde6736b4876b8597985ca0bd55614ddb8dd285aa356c848494eaaaed30e4811" data-url="/nats-io/nats-server/pull/7609/partials/commit_status_icon?oid=5a98bca675f6383957ab96df83502f0b5885bad2">

  <div class="commit-build-statuses">
    <span class="Skeleton d-inline-block" style="width:12px; height:12px;"></span>
  </div>

</batch-deferred-content>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="text-right ml-1">
        <code>
          <a href="/nats-io/nats-server/pull/7609/commits/5a98bca675f6383957ab96df83502f0b5885bad2" class="Link--secondary">5a98bca</a>
        </code>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
    <div class="Details-content--hidden mt-2">
      <pre class="color-fg-muted ws-pre-wrap">Fix the case where a node that removes all of the peers would be
left with `membChanging` set permanently. This would prevent the
single node cluster to admit new nodes back to form a bigger
cluster. The `membChanging` flag prevents concurrent membership
changes, and is reset after a membership change is committed.
However, in the case of a single node cluster, entries are
never committed and `memChanging` remains set.
The underlying problem is that single node clusters are not
working properly: entries are committed only after tracking
successful append entry responses from the followers. In a single
node cluster there's no one that sends append entry responses,
and the quorum checking is never done elsewhere.
The fix consists in extracting function `tryCommit` from
`trackResponse`. `tryCommit` checks if there's a quorum for a
given index, and if so it commits the corresponding entry. We
call this function if, after proposing a peer remove, we are
left with a single node cluster.

Signed-off-by: Daniele Sciascia &lt;daniele@nats.io&gt;</pre>
    </div>
</div>

</div>
</div>  </div>
</div>


</div>

      <div class="js-timeline-item js-timeline-progressive-focus-container" data-gid="HRFPE_lADOAGJRq87cJtPnzwAAAAT424HO">
